				const currentStylesheet = document.querySelector( '.tcb-lightspeed-style:not([data-ls-optimized])' )

				if ( currentStylesheet ) {
					try {
						if ( currentStylesheet.sheet && currentStylesheet.sheet.cssRules ) {
							if ( window.flatStyles ) {
								if ( this.optimizing ) {
									setTimeout( window.lightspeedOptimizeStylesheet.bind( this ), 24 )
								} else {
									this.optimizing = true;

									let rulesIndex = 0;

									while ( rulesIndex < currentStylesheet.sheet.cssRules.length ) {
										const rule = currentStylesheet.sheet.cssRules[ rulesIndex ]
										/* remove rules that already exist in the page */
										if ( rule.type === CSSRule.STYLE_RULE && window.flatStyles.includes( `${rule.selectorText}{` ) ) {

										}
									}
									/* optimize, mark it such, move to the next file, append the styles we have until now */
									currentStylesheet.setAttribute( 'data-ls-optimized', '1' )

									window.flatStyles += currentStylesheet.innerHTML

							} else {

					}

						const stylesheetID = currentStylesheet.id;
						/**

						if ( ( ! stylesheetID || ( stylesheetID && ! document.querySelector( `head #${stylesheetID}` ) ) ) ) {

						}
					}
				}
			}

				if ( document.querySelectorAll( 'link[href*="thrive_flat.css"]' ).length > 1 ) {

					styleSheetElement.setAttribute( 'disabled', true )
				} else {

@font-face{ 
font-family:'Neutral400';
	src:url(/wp-content/uploads/Neutral-800.woff2) format('woff2');
	unicode-range:U+000-5FF;
	font-weight:800;
	font-style:normal;
	font-display:swap;
 }

@font-face{ 
font-family:'Neutral400';
	src:url(/wp-content/uploads/Neutral-700.woff2) format('woff2');
	unicode-range:U+000-5FF;
	font-weight:700;
	font-style:normal;
	font-display:swap;
 }

@font-face{ 
font-family:'Neutral400';
	src:url(/wp-content/uploads/Neutral-400-Italic.woff2) format('woff2');
	unicode-range:U+000-5FF;
	font-weight:400;
	font-style:italic;
	font-display:swap;
 }

/* Mega Menu  */

.thrv_widget_menu.thrv_wrapper ul.tve_w_menu li.menu-item>a{
	transition: color 0.2s ease;
}

.tve-regular.tcb-mega-std .thrv_text_element > p{
	font-family:Neutral500!important;
	color:#888888!important;
}

.tve-m-trigger .tcb-icon-close > svg{
	color:#505050!important;
}

.tve-m-trigger .tcb-icon-close > svg:hover {
	color:#fcc500!important;
}

span.tcb-mm-text span.tve-disabled-text-inner{
	font-family:Neutral700;
	transition: all 0.2s ease;
	color:#606060;
}

span.tcb-mm-text span.tve-disabled-text-inner:hover{
	color:#fcc500;
}

/* Thrive Leads Close Button */

.tve_ea_thrive_leads_form_close{
	right:-9px!important;
	top:-30px!important;
}

/* Breadcrumb */

.thrive-breadcrumbs li.thrive-breadcrumb-leaf span{
	text-align:left;
}

.thrive-breadcrumbs li a{
	transition: all 0.2s ease;
}

/* Archive Posts */ 

.tcb-post-list h2 span a {
	color:#606060!important;
	transition: all 0.2s ease!important;
}

.tcb-post-list h2 span a:hover {
	color:#909090!important;
}

.tcb-pagination-button a, .tcb-pagination-number {
	transition: all 0.2s ease!important;
}

/* Post Content */

body:not(.woocommerce-page) .tcb-post-content p {
	text-align:justify;
}

.tcb-post-content p.has-drop-cap {
	margin-top:5px;
}

.tcb-post-content strong, .tcb-post-content strong a{
	font-weight:700;
}

.tcb-post-content code{
	font-family:monospace;
	font-size:0.95rem;
	color:inherit;
}

body:not(.woocommerce-page) .tcb-post-content a:not(.tcb-button-link), .tcb-post-content p a, .tcb-post-content li a, .thrive_author_box a {
	transition:all 0.3s ease;
	color:#72990a;
}

body:not(.woocommerce-page)  .tcb-post-content.thrv_wrapper p a:hover, .tcb-post-content.thrv_wrapper li a:hover, .thrive_author_box a:hover {
	color:#6a8a16;
	font-weight:inherit;
	text-decoration:underline;
}

.tcb-post-content ul ul, .tcb-post-content ul ol, .tcb-post-content ol ul, .tcb-post-content ol ol {
	padding:4px 0;
}

.thrv_wrapper.tcb-post-content ul:not(.wcs-tabs-nav) li:not(.thrv-styled-list-item){
	position:relative;
	margin-left:auto;
	left:1em;
	padding-left:4px;
	padding-right:1em;
}

.thrv_wrapper.tcb-post-content ol li{
	position:relative;
	left:1.5em;
	margin-left:auto;
	padding-left:4px;
	padding-right:1em;
}

.thrv-button a.tcb-button-link {
	transition: all 0.2s ease;
}

button:not(.single_add_to_cart_button button) {
	transition: all 0.2s ease;
}

.wp-block-button{
	margin:15px 0;
}

.tcb-post-content .wp-block-button .wp-block-button__link {
	text-decoration:none;
	transition:all 0.2s ease;
	padding:15px 25px;
	font-weight:700;
}

.wp-block-button:not(.is-style-outline) .wp-block-button__link:hover {
  filter:brightness(95%);
}

.wp-block-columns {
	margin: 0px 0 0px 0;
}

.wp-block-image {
	margin: 0px 0;
}

.tcb-post-content figure {
	margin: 0;
}

.tcb-post-content .alignright, .tcb-post-content .alignleft {
	margin-top:0;
}

.tcb-post-content img {
	padding: 15px 0;
	transition:all 0.2s ease;
}

.tcb-post-content.thrv_wrapper img:hover {
	opacity:0.8;
}

figure figcaption{
	margin-top:0px!important;
	margin-bottom:10px!important;
	text-align:center;
	padding:0 20px;
}

div#ez-toc-container {
	margin:15px auto;
	width:auto;
	max-width:450px;
}

div#ez-toc-container p.ez-toc-title {
	padding:10px 0 10px 10%;
	text-align:center;
	line-height:1.25em;
}

ul.ez-toc-list li{
	left:2px!important;
}

ul.ez-toc-list-level-1 {
	padding:0px 0px 10px 10px!important;
}

body:not(.woocommerce-page) .tcb-post-content table {
	margin:15px 0;
}

body:not(.woocommerce-page) .tcb-post-content table tr td {
	padding:0.5em 1em!important;
	border:solid 1px #cccccc;
}

body:not(.woocommerce-page) .tcb-post-content table th{
	padding:15px 0;
	border:solid 1px #cccccc!important;
	background-color:#606060;
	color:#fcfcfc;
	transition:all 0.2s ease!important;
}

body:not(.woocommerce-page) .tcb-post-content table th:hover{
	filter:brightness(95%);
	color:#fcc500;
}

body:not(.woocommerce-page) .tcb-post-content table tr td{
	transition: all 0.2s ease;
}

body:not(.woocommerce-page) .tcb-post-content table tr td:hover{
		filter:brightness(96.5%);
}

body:not(.woocommerce-page) .tcb-post-content table tr:nth-child(odd) td {
	background:#fefefe;
}

body:not(.woocommerce-page) .tcb-post-content table tr:nth-child(even) td {
	background-color:#f7f7f7;
}

.essb-ctt {
	margin: 15px auto;
	padding: 30px 25px 15px 25px;
}

.mediatext {
	margin:10px 0;
	padding:10px 20px;
}

.tcb-post-list > article > .tve-default-heading > p > span {
	transition:all 0.2s ease;
	color:#808080;
}

.tcb-post-list > article:hover > .tve-default-heading > p > span {
	color:#606060;
}

/* Hub Content */

.tve-theme-30709 h2:not(.normal-heading) {
	color:#606060;
	border:1.5px solid #606060;
	border-left:7px solid #606060;
	padding:10px 0 10px 2.1rem;
	margin:25px 0 10px 0;
	background-color:rgba(246,246,246,1);
}

.tve-theme-30709 h2:not(.normal-heading)::before{
  content:'';
	position:relative;
	left:-.9rem;
	vertical-align:middle;
	background-image:url(/wp-content/uploads/Network-Icon.png);
	background-repeat:no-repeat;
	background-size:contain;
	display:inline-block;
	width:1.6rem;
	height:1.8rem;
}

.sidebar-title .tve-content-box-background{
	transition:all 0.2s ease!important;
}

.sidebar-title a {
	font-family:Neutral700!important;
	color:#fcfcfc!important;
}

.sidebar-link {
	line-height:1.5em!important;
}

.sidebar-link a, .subpages a {
	font-family:Neutral700!important;
	transition: all 0.2s ease!important;
  color:#909090!important;
}

.sidebar-link a:hover, .subpages a:hover{
	font-family:Neutral700;
	color:#606060!important;
	text-decoration:none!important;
}

.subpages .page_item{
	list-style:none;
}

.subpages .page_item::before {
	content:"\1F50D";
	font-size:15px;
	display:inline-block;
	width:29px;
	margin-left:-20px;
}


/* Custom Classes */

.white-link a{
	font-family:Neutral500!important;
	color:#fcfcfc!important;
}

.dropcap-intro-card {
	padding:20px 25px 15px 0;
	margin:10px 0 15px 0;
}

.colorbox {
	margin: 10px 0;
	padding: 20px 30px;
	transition:all 0.2s ease;
}

div.colorbox:hover{
	filter: brightness(92.5%);
}

p.colorbox-title, p.colorbox-title strong{
	font-size:20px;
	font-family:Neutral800, sans-serif;
	padding:5px 0;
	color:inherit;
}

.colorbox p{
		color:inherit;
		padding:5px 0;
}

.titlebox {
	margin: 15px 0;
	width:100%;
	border: 2px solid;
	transition:all 0.2s ease;
}

.titlebox:hover{
	opacity:0.9;
}

p.titlebox-title{
	font-family:Neutral500;
	text-align:left!important;
	font-size:20px;
	line-height:1.4em;
	color:inherit;
	padding: 10px 25px;
}

.titlebox-content{
	color:#505050;
	background-color:#fcfcfc;
	padding:15px 25px;
}

.titlebox-content p{
	padding:5px 0;
}

.iframe {
	width:100%;
	min-height:400px;
	margin: 15px 0;
}

.fullwidth, .fullwidth a {
	width:100%;
	box-sizing:border-box;
}

.skyblue, .skyblue-hover .wp-block-button__link:hover {
	background-color:#008fff;
	border-color:#008fff;
	color:#fcfcfc;
}

.navyblue {
	background-color:#007ed8;
	border-color:#007ed8;
	color:#fcfcfc;
}

.olive, .olive-hover .wp-block-button__link:hover{
	background-color:#6a8a16!important;
	border-color:#6a8a16;
	color:#fcfcfc!important;
}

.freshgreen{
	background-color:#99cc00;
	border-color:#99cc00;
	color:#fcfcfc;
}

.khaki{
	background: #f0e68c;
	border-color:#f0e68c;
	color: #505050;
}

.golden{
	background-color:#fcc500;
	border-color:#fcc500;
	color:#fcfcfc;
}

.orange, .orange-hover .wp-block-button__link:hover {
	background-color:#ff9900;
	border-color:#ff9900;
	color:#fcfcfc;
}

.tangerine {
	background-color:#ff7a23;
	border-color:#ff7a23;
	color:#fcfcfc;
}

.brown {
	background-color:#7b5438;
	border-color:#7b5438;
	color:#fcfcfc;
}

.red {
	background-color:#aa0000;
	border-color:#aa0000;
	color:#fcfcfc;
}

.purple {
	background-color:#813c96;
	border-color:#813c96;
	color:#fcfcfc;
}

/* WC Accordion & Tab */

.wc-shortcodes-tabs{
	margin:10px 0;
}

.wc-shortcodes-tabs .wcs-tabs-nav a{
	transition:all 0.2s ease;
	font-family:Neutral700, sans-serif;
}

.wc-shortcodes-tabs .wcs-tabs-nav a:hover{
	text-decoration:none!important;
	background-color:#6f8b28!important;
	color:#fcfcfc!important;
}

.wc-shortcodes-tabs .wcs-tabs-nav .wcs-state-active a{
	background-color:#6f8b28!important;
	color:#fcfcfc!important;
}

.wc-shortcodes-tabs .tab-content{
	padding: 15px 25px;
}

.wc-shortcodes-accordion {
	margin: 0;
}

.wc-shortcodes-item{
	margin:15px 0;
}

.wc-shortcodes-accordion .wc-shortcodes-accordion-content{
	padding:10px 20px;
}

.wc-shortcodes-accordion .wc-shortcodes-accordion-trigger a {
	color:#72990a;
	transition:all 0.2s ease;
	font-family:Neutral700, sans-serif;
}

.wc-shortcodes-accordion .wc-shortcodes-accordion-trigger a:hover, .wc-shortcodes-accordion .wc-shortcodes-accordion-header-active a {
	text-decoration:none!important;
	background-color:#6f8b28!important;
	color:#fcfcfc!important;
}

/* Previous and Next Links */

.pnav {
	margin:20px 0;
	display:flex;	
}

.pnav .pav {
	border:1px solid #dcdcdc;
	box-sizing: border-box;
	width:50%;
	transition: background 0.3s ease;
  padding: 30px 30px;
  display: table-cell;
}

.pnav .pav:hover {
	text-decoration:none;
}

.pnav .pav p:first-child{
	color:#777777;
	padding-top:10px;
	padding-bottom:5px;
	font-family:Neutral500;
}

.pnav .pav:hover{
	background-color:#606060;
}

.pnav .pav:hover p:first-child{
		color:#fcfcfc;
}

.pnav .pav:hover p:nth-child(2){
		color:#ffcc00;
}

.pnav .pav p:nth-child(2){
	font-size:18px;
	line-height:1.4em;
	font-family:Neutral700;
}

.pnav .pav.left {
	padding-right:40px;
	float:left;
}

.pnav .pav.left p{
		text-align:right;
}

.pnav .pav.right {
	padding-left:40px;
	float:right;
	text-align:left;
}

.pnav .pav.right p{
		text-align:left;
}

@media only screen and (max-width: 540px){
.pnav .pav.left, .pnav .pav.right{
		padding:8.5% 6%;
}
	.pnav .pav p:first-child{
		padding-top:5px;
		padding-bottom:15px;
	}
}

/* Author Box */

.thrive_author_box strong {
	font-weight: 700;
}

/* Post Comment Form */

.comment-form>p input[type='email'], .comment-form>p input[type='text'], .comment-form>p input[type='url']{
	padding: 0 10px;
}

.comment-form-item.comment-form-url{
	margin-bottom:5px;
}

#wp-comment-fc-consent {
	margin-right:6px;
}

.content-section .comment-form .comment-form-submit button{
	transition:all 0.2s ease;
}

/* Contact Form */

.thrv-contact-form .tve-cf-submit>button{
	transition: all 0.2s ease;
}

/* Footer */

footer.thrv_footer a {
	transition: all 0.2s ease!important;
}

/* WooCommerce Product Page */

.woocommerce div.product h1.product_title {
	font-weight:400;
}

.woocommerce div.product p.price, .single-product .woocommerce div.product span.price{
	color: #505050;
	margin-bottom:5px;
}

.woocommerce ul.products li.product .price del, .woocommerce ul.products li.product .price .woocommerce-price-suffix {
	font-size:17px;
	font-family:'Neutral500', sans-serif;
}

.woocommerce ul.products li.product .price ins {
	font-size:17px;
	font-family:'Neutral800', sans-serif;
}

.woocommerce div.product form.cart .button{
	color:#505050;
}

.woocommerce div.product .woocommerce-tabs ul.tabs{
	margin-bottom:15px;
}

.single-product .woocommerce-product-details__short-description{
	margin-bottom:10px;
}

.product-template-wrapper>.product>.woocommerce-tabs.wc-tabs-wrapper h2{
	margin-bottom:0;
}

.woocommerce ul.products li.product .woocommerce-loop-product__title {
	line-height:1.4em;
	text-align:center;
}

.woocommerce ul.products li.product .price {
	margin-top:12px;
	text-align:center!important;
}

.woocommerce ul.products li.product .button{
	font-family:'Neutral800', sans-serif;
	margin-left:auto;
	margin-right:auto;
	letter-spacing:-0.5px;
}

/* Woocommerce Cart Page */

.woocommerce div.woocommerce-message, .error404 div.woocommerce-message{
	line-height:1.5em;
}

.woocommerce table.shop_table{
	margin: 15px 0;
}

.woocommerce-cart table, .woocommerce-cart th, .woocommerce-cart td {
	border:none;
}

body.woocommerce-cart h2{
	font-size:24px;
}

@media (min-width: 768px){
.woocommerce-cart td.product-name {
	max-width:300px;
}
}

.woocommerce-cart .paypal-buttons.paypal-buttons-context-iframe.paypal-buttons-label-paypal.paypal-buttons-layout-horizontal{
	display:block!important;
	margin: 20px auto 20px auto;
}

#add_payment_method .wc-proceed-to-checkout a.checkout-button, .woocommerce-cart .wc-proceed-to-checkout a.checkout-button, .woocommerce-checkout .wc-proceed-to-checkout a.checkout-button{
	color:#505050;
}

.woocommerce-cart .wc-proceed-to-checkout a.checkout-button:hover{
	color:#707070;
}

.woocommerce .cart-collaterals .cross-sells, .woocommerce-page .cart-collaterals .cross-sells {
	width:100%;
}

.woocommerce .cart-collaterals .cross-sells ul.products, .woocommerce-page .cart-collaterals .cross-sells ul.products {
	margin-left:0px!important;
}

.woocommerce .cart-collaterals .cross-sells ul.products li, .woocommerce-page .cart-collaterals .cross-sells ul.products li {
	left:0;
	padding:0 0;
}

.woocommerce .cart-collaterals .cross-sells ul.products li.product a img {
	margin-top:10px;
	margin-bottom:0;
}

.woocommerce .cart-collaterals .cross-sells ul.products li, .woocommerce-page .cart-collaterals .cross-sells ul.products li .button:hover{
	font-weight:700;
}

.button.wc-backward {
	margin-bottom:30px!important;
}

.button.wc-backward:hover {
	font-family:Neutral700;
}

/* WooCommerce Checkout Page */

.woocommerce-checkout .woocommerce-error::before{
	position:absolute!important;
	top: 20px;
	left: 20px;
}

.woocommerce-checkout ul.woocommerce-error{
	margin-left:0!important;
	padding:15px 45px!important;
}

.woocommerce-checkout ul.woocommerce-error li{
	line-height:1.5em;
}

.woocommerce-checkout .woocommerce-info {
	margin-bottom:5px;
}

.woocommerce form.checkout_coupon, .woocommerce form.login{
	margin-top:0!important;
	margin-bottom:15px;
}

.woocommerce-checkout .woocommerce-input-wrapper > #_fc_woo_checkout_subscribe{
  display: none;
}

.woocommerce-checkout .woocommerce-form-login .woocommerce-form-login__rememberme{
	padding-bottom:15px;
}

.woocommerce-checkout #payment div.payment_box{
	padding-bottom:5px;
}

.woocommerce-checkout div.payment_method_stripe {
	padding-top:5px!important;
}

.woocommerce-checkout #payment div.payment_box .form-row{
	padding: 5px 5px;
	margin: 5px 5px;
}

.woocommerce-checkout #payment ul.payment_methods li div.payment_method_stripe input {
	margin-right:	5px;
}

.woocommerce-checkout form .form-row.validate-required {
	margin-top:10px;
	margin-bottom:10px;
}

.woocommerce-checkout .woocommerce-form__label-for-checkbox{
	padding-top:5px;
	line-height:1.5em!important;
}

.woocommerce-checkout form .form-row label{
	padding-bottom:5px;
	line-height:1.5;
}

.woocommerce #payment #place_order, .woocommerce-page #payment #place_order{
	margin-top:10px;
	color:#505050;
}

.woocommerce-checkout #payment{
	padding-bottom:5px;
}

.woocommerce-checkout .paypal-powered-by{
	display:none!important;
}

/* Woocommerce Account Page */

.woocommerce-account .woocommerce-MyAccount-navigation a{
	font-family:Neutral500, sans-serif;
	line-height:1.5em;
	padding: 5px 20px 7px 45px;
}

.woocommerce-account span.product-title:hover {
	font-weight:700;
}

.widget.woocommerce .widget-title{
	padding-bottom:20px;
}</style></head><body class="wp-singular post-template-default single single-post postid-7485 single-format-standard wp-theme-thrive-theme theme-thrive-theme woocommerce-no-js wc-shortcodes-font-awesome-enabled tve-theme-34432 thrive-layout-34421 essb-10.3"> <script>!function(f,b,e,v,n,t,s)

normal;">Desmos: A Definitive Guide on Graphing and Computing</span></h1></div><div class="thrv_wrapper thrv_text_element" style="" data-css="tve-u-16ed0d75200"><p data-css="tve-u-16ed0d79075" content-section"><div class="section-background"></div><div class="section-content"><section class="tcb-post-content tcb-shortcode thrv_wrapper" data-css="tve-u-16ec5d248bb"><figure><img loading="lazy" decoding="async" class="wp-image-7651 aligncenter" src="https://mathvault.ca/wp-content/uploads/Desmos-Post.jpg" alt="Desmos: A Definitive Guide to Graphing and Computing" width="800" height="480" title="Desmos Post"></figure><p style="text-align: justify;">Think you&#8217;re fond of of <em>graphing</em> and <em>computing</em> stuffs? Great! Because you might remember this thing called the <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/TI-83_series" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Texas Instrument TI-83</strong></a> from the old days. Sure, while programmable calculators in general are still pretty much popular these days, the graphing calculators from the 21<sup>st</sup>-century are also coming in waves as we speak — potentially disrupting the market of <em>scientific computing</em> and <em>educational technology</em>.</p><p style="text-align: justify;">In particular, there&#8217;s a certain <em>education startup</em> out there, relentlessly seeking to hijack our <em>Internet browsers</em> and <em>mobile devices</em> into a — should we say — graphing extravaganza. And it comes with a funky name as well: <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Desmos_(graphing)" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Desmos</strong></a>.</p><p style="text-align: justify;">Yep. You&#8217;ve heard it right. As <em>Greek-mythology-inspired</em> as it sounds, Desmos actually has nothing to do with the giant monster responsible for turning <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mount_Olympus" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Mount Olympus</a> into rubble through the wrath of infernal flames. Instead, it is probably better known as an innocent-looking math tool for the scientifically-minded, making applied math ever more palatable and entertaining!</p><p style="text-align: justify;">Oh. Don&#8217;t believe in the<em> mighty power</em> of Desmos? Good job! Because it&#8217;ll then be our duty to beg to differ, and attempt to convince you otherwise. Indeed, if at the end of the module you still find the scope of Desmos&#8217; functionalities <em>unappealing</em>, then — and only then — shall we concede defeat and return to our ivory tower for more advanced Buddhist meditation training! On the other hand, if you&#8217;re just way too lazy to read the 12-page <a href="https://s3.amazonaws.com/desmos/Desmos_Calculator_User_Guide.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Desmos user manual</strong></a>, and are looking for more <em>concrete examples</em> to kick-start the creative process, then this one is for you too!<span id="more-7485"></span></p><div id="toc"><div id="ez-toc-container" class="ez-toc-v2_0_82_1 counter-hierarchy ez-toc-counter ez-toc-custom ez-toc-container-direction"><div class="ez-toc-title-container"><p class="ez-toc-title ez-toc-toggle" style="cursor:pointer">Table of Contents</p>

class="ez-toc-js-icon-con"><span class=""><span class="eztoc-hide" style="display:none;">Toggle</span><span class="ez-toc-icon-toggle-span"><svg style="fill: #000000;color:#000000" 6.2-6.3c.2-.2.3-.5.3-.7s-.1-.5-.3-.7c-.2-.2-.4-.3-.7-.3h-11c-.3 0-.5.1-.7.3-.2.2-.3.5-.3.7s.1.5.3.7z"/></svg></span></span></span></a></span></div><nav><ul class='ez-toc-list ez-toc-list-level-1 ' href="#Definite_Integrals" >Definite Integrals</a></li></ul></li><li class='ez-toc-page-1 ez-toc-heading-level-2'><a class="ez-toc-link ez-toc-heading-18" href="#Statistics_with_Desmos" >Statistics with Desmos</a><ul class='ez-toc-list-level-3' ><li class='ez-toc-heading-level-3'><a class="ez-toc-link ez-toc-heading-19" href="#Tables" >Tables</a></li><li class='ez-toc-page-1 ez-toc-heading-21" href="#Regression" >Regression</a></li></ul></li><li class='ez-toc-page-1 ez-toc-heading-level-2'><a class="ez-toc-link ez-toc-heading-22" href="#Miscellaneous"
